### 2026-08-01 09:22:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Credit Rating #Importance 1

Czech Republic Credit Ratings Affirm at AA- with Stable Outlook — Fitch Ratings affirmed the Czech Republic’s long-term foreign- and local-currency issuer default ratings at ‘AA-’ with a Stable Outlook, highlighting credible macroeconomic policies, strong institutions, and a solid external balance sheet. Fiscal deficits are set to widen under the new coalition’s looser stance, but Fitch expects easing to remain contained, keeping debt below peer medians. Growth prospects are robust, supporting income convergence with richer EU members, though risks arise from the government’s confrontational tone toward the EU on migration and climate policy. EU fund disbursements are not seen as jeopardized, and overall resilience persists despite fiscal pressures. Standard & Poor's credit rating for Czech Republic stands at AA- with stable outlook. Moody's credit rating for Czech Republic was last set at Aa3 with stable outlook, and DBRS' credit rating was last reported at AA with stable outlook.

【五糧液普五八代五連陰 白酒大單品總價跌回近十天低點】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，中國白酒市場主要大單品的終端零售總價8月1日低位微跌。如果主要單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為9871元，較昨日小幅下跌1元，再平7月30日創下的近十天低點。縱觀全場，過去的一天酒市終端交投情緒低位趨弱，多空雙方繼續拉鋸，總價窄幅盤整格局尚未打破。

今日11大白酒單品五漲五跌一平，贏家與輸家數量平分秋色，但下跌品種的合計跌幅略勝一籌。上漲方面，習酒君品漲幅居前，上漲5元/瓶，終結此前三連跌；古井貢古20上漲4元/瓶，結束連續三日走低，短線反彈再度啟動；青花汾20與國窖1573均上漲3元/瓶，其中前者錄得二連陽，重返近一個月次高位，後者連續兩日自今年2月2日以來最低位反彈；精品茅臺小漲1元/瓶，昨日回落後今日轉漲，價格繼續在2400元下方盤整。下跌方面，五糧液普五八代與洋河夢之藍M6+並列領跌，均下跌6元/瓶。其中，前者遭遇五連陰，累計下跌28元/瓶，價格降至770元/瓶，重新整理“酒價內參”自去年11月有統計以來最低價紀錄，近期低位加速探底態勢延續。飛天茅臺下跌3元/瓶，7.18提價後的高位整理格局延續；五糧液1618與水晶劍南春均小跌1元/瓶。青花郎價格環比昨日保持不變。

【日元受干預行動提振上漲 訊息稱美日兩國同步進場買入日元】週五美日兩國當局持續出手託舉日元，日元兌美元、歐元匯率漲幅均超1%。 知情人士透露，日本當局週五在紐約交易時段進場買日元、賣美元。日經新聞早前報道，日本政府與日本央行連續第二日實施日元干預；另有媒體訊息稱，紐約聯邦儲備銀行代表美國財政部拋售歐元、買入日元。 該報表示，這是美日兩國近30年來首次聯合干預，共同支撐日元。美國財政部尚未就相關報道予以回應；日本財務省官員暫未置評。 兩名知情人士此前透露，紐約聯儲日內至少聯絡兩家美國大型銀行，詢盤歐元兌日元報價。連日來美日相繼採取行動，體現雙方協同遏制日元貶值。上週日元觸及1986年以來低點。油價走高、日本持續財政赤字，疊加日本與美國等主要經濟體巨大利差，持續壓制日元匯率。 道明證券新加坡經濟學家Alex Loo表示：“交易員將會警惕當局後續再度實施干預。”

【Reuters photo shows Bessent notebook 'buy yen $5-10bln'; FT says U.S., Japan intervened, NY Fed sold euros to buy yen via Goldman, Morgan Stanley】A Reuters photo shows Treasury Secretary Bessent's notebook containing the note "buy yen $5-10bln." The Financial Times reported the U.S. intervened on Friday in coordination with Japan, marking the first joint direct yen purchase in nearly 30 years. Three people said the New York Fed, acting for the Treasury, executed an unusual operation — selling euros to buy yen — and two said the trades were routed through Goldman Sachs and Morgan Stanley. A Treasury spokesman declined to comment on the notebook or whether intervention took place; Treasury had notified several Wall Street banks it was considering action. The last U.S. operation to support the yen was in 2011, when it sold yen.

【美媒：美聯儲主席考慮減少每年議息會議頻次】美國媒體7月31日援引知情人士的話報道說，美國聯邦儲備委員會主席凱文·沃什正在考慮減少每年常規貨幣政策會議（即議息會議）召開頻次。《紐約時報》援引4名知情人士的話說，沃什在本週議息會議上提議減少這類會議的召開頻次，談及規定議息會議每年最少召開頻次的法律以及要對此作出調整的時間框架，但沒有對“會議日曆”進行充分討論，而是要求美聯儲官員向他反饋想法。根據美國《1935年銀行法》規定，美聯儲聯邦公開市場委員會每年應至少召開4次議息會議。自1981年以來，美聯儲每年固定召開8次議息會議，大約每6週一次。（新華社）

【國際足聯主席：叫停世界盃股權出售計劃】據英國天空新聞頻道今天（8月1日）報道，國際足聯主席因凡蒂諾已“放棄出售世界盃等國際足聯賽事部分股權的計劃”。報道稱，天空新聞獨家獲取的宣告中，因凡蒂諾表示：“在仔細聽取各方意見後，我意識到該專案引發了嚴重分歧。無論支援率高低，這種局面已不再符合我們最初的既定目標。我們的宗旨始終是——也將永遠是——團結各方、謀求進步。因此，該提案將不再推進。” 宣告還稱，因凡蒂諾“計劃在接下來的數週乃至數月內，本著對足球運動的共同關切，召集所有利益相關方重新磋商，致力於在全球範圍內持續發展足球事業，尤其為那些最需要支援的國家的足球發展提供更多助力。” 因凡蒂諾此前計劃成立一家價值200億美元的子公司來運營世界盃及其他賽事，並向私人投資者出售少數股權。 但這一提案遭到以歐足聯為首的多個大洲足聯的堅決反對。歐足聯7月30日宣佈抵制包括世界盃在內的所有國際足聯賽事。因凡蒂諾的高階顧問也因此辭職。（CCTV國際時訊）

【經濟日報刊文：夯實中國電影“走出去”的底氣】高分熱映國產動畫電影《八仙！》片方近日正式公佈海外上映檔期。作為立足中華傳統神話故事的原創動畫電影，影片採用適配全球全年齡層觀眾的敘事視角，同步面向世界不同文化圈層鋪開發行，覆蓋歐美、大洋洲、東南亞等多國多地。來自中國的“八仙”，將以全新的方式再次“過海”，帶給世界又一次東方美學的體驗。從行業發展來看，電影是驅動文化產業前行的核心引擎。中國電影在海外持續圈粉，以優質內容、龐大市場、產業創新實現行業領跑。根據國家電影局釋出的資料，2025年，中國電影年度海外票房突破10億元，海外上映國家和地區達到46個，年度海外票房收入超過百萬美元的影片達到13部，國產電影票房前20位的影片有15部實現了海外上映。《哪吒之魔童鬧海》單片海外票房（含港澳市場）達6900萬美元，創下近年來國產影片單片海外票房新高。這些數字背後，是中國電影系統性“出海”步伐的加快。從內容創作到製作技術，從發行渠道到市場運營，全產業鏈能力的持續提升為中國電影參與全球競爭奠定了堅實基礎。影視技術的創新突圍，讓中國從單純的內容供給者，進階為全球影視行業標準制定的參與者；產業發展與政策扶持的雙重紅利，讓中國電影完成由“文化輸出”向深度參與全球產業的跨越。

### 2026-08-01 06:41:06  #其他

【經濟日報：康養旅遊並非旅遊養生簡單疊加】隨著人們健康意識的提高，康養旅遊正成為越來越多消費者的選擇。近日印發的《旅遊強國建設“十五五”規劃》提出，推動旅遊與醫藥等領域有機融合、雙向賦能，積極培育康養旅遊等融合業態。這為各地打造高品質康養旅遊專案提供了清晰思路。康養旅遊的興起，是經濟社會發展和旅遊產業變革共同作用的結果。一方面，我國人口老齡化程度持續加深，銀髮群體對健康養生、情感陪伴和興趣社交的需求日益旺盛；另一方面，大眾旅遊已由單一的觀光打卡向沉浸式休閒體驗迭代，兼具休閒度假、養生健體等多種功能的康養旅遊，正在成為文旅消費新藍海。也要看到，發展康養旅遊並非將旅遊與養生概念簡單疊加，而是要立足資源稟賦、深耕特色內涵，因地制宜推動康養旅遊融合發展。可立足本土特色優勢，發揮各地森林、溫泉、中醫藥等多種資源優勢，積極打造森林氧吧康養、溫泉理療、藥膳養生等多種型別產品，形成多元互補的供給體系。

【美一架F35隱形戰機墜毀】據美國海軍陸戰隊通報，當地時間7月31日上午10點左右，在美國加利福尼亞州的米拉馬爾航空站附近，美國海軍陸戰隊一架F-35B隱形戰鬥機墜毀。飛行員被送往醫療機構救治，具體傷情不明。據美國媒體報道，涉事戰機隸屬於第3海軍陸戰隊航空聯隊第11大隊。 目擊者稱，這架戰機在準備降落時“飛得極低，錯過了跑道”，飛行員彈射逃生，隨後飛機“爆出一團巨大的火球”。據目擊者描述，飛機觸地時“尾部貼近地面，機頭則向上翹”。 美國軍方正在調查事故原因。 海軍陸戰隊在宣告中稱此次事件為“A級事故”。據悉，這是最嚴重的事故等級，適用於損失金額超過200萬美元、軍用航空器損毀或現役軍人死亡的情況。 F-35B是美軍“閃電II”家族中具備短距起飛與垂直降落能力的型號，主要裝備美國海軍陸戰隊，造價約1.1億美元。（CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-08-01 05:00:04  #公司

【AI敘事降溫 “算力金屬”還能漲嗎】過去兩年，在AI浪潮推動下，雲廠商、晶片及相關資源品價格一路水漲船高。如今，隨著AI敘事逐步進入深水區，市場已經不滿足於僅交易遠期預期，而希望看到AI帶來的實際利潤。 這一變化，也讓此前漲勢凌厲的“算力金屬”面臨重新審視。全球科技巨頭陸續披露財報，微軟、Meta、谷歌母公司Alphabet仍在投入鉅額資金建設AI基礎設施，但自由現金流承壓、資本開支增長快於收入回報等問題，也讓投資者重新評估這場AI競賽的可持續性。與此同時，光互連、高壓直流供電等新架構加速落地，也可能降低部分環節的單位算力銅耗。銅之外，錫、鍺等更多“算力金屬”也面臨著類似的問題。 AI對銅需求的拉動是否被高估？業內人士認為，答案並非簡單的是或否，AI用銅邏輯已經綜合了對實際投產規模、技術路線變化和電力配套進度的判斷。機房內部的單位用銅量可能下降，但資料中心擴張帶來的電源、變電、輸配電和冷卻需求仍在增長。（中證報）

Brent Surges 24% in July — Brent crude oil prices rose 1.2% to near $88 a barrel on Friday, posting a nearly 24% gain in July, its strongest monthly increase since March, driven by escalating geopolitical tensions and growing concerns over global oil supplies. Iran claimed it attacked two tankers transiting the Strait of Hormuz under US military escort, though Western maritime authorities have not confirmed the incident. Renewed conflict between the US and Iran, Houthi attacks in the Red Sea, and Saudi strikes on Iran-backed groups have heightened risks to key shipping routes. Falling US crude inventories have added further upward pressure on prices. Meanwhile, attacks near Russia’s Black Sea oil export infrastructure, including the Caspian Pipeline Consortium (CPC) terminal, have raised concerns about disruptions to Kazakhstan’s oil exports, an important source for European refiners. Although the CPC decided to continue operations, uncertainty over supply security continues to support higher oil prices.

【報道：沃什考慮減少美聯儲政策會議的頻率】凱文・M・沃什正考慮減少美聯儲制定利率的例行會議場次。此舉可能引發重大震動，或將成為這家央行多年來運營模式最重大的改革。美聯儲由 12 名成員組成的政策委員會每年召開八次會議，投票決定上調、下調或是維持借貸成本。四名獲准匿名受訪的知情人士透露，沃什在美聯儲本週的會議上提出調整上述會議頻次的構想。這是沃什以主席身份主持的第二次會議。他釋放訊號，美聯儲有望在 9 月中旬下次會議前敲定新會議日程方案，相關調整或延後落地實施。美聯儲方面拒絕置評。下調會議頻次，隨之減少利率投票次數，將是沃什上任至今影響最為深遠的舉措。該方案將打破延續數十年的慣例，重塑美聯儲調控經濟的方式，還可能削弱其針對通脹、就業市場變動做出快速應對的能力。會議次數減少，華爾街與公眾能夠獲取的美聯儲利率思路相關資訊也會變少，扭轉持續數十年的資訊透明化趨勢。

### 2026-08-01 03:51:28  #公司 #國際

【專家：Anthropic與OpenAI入侵事件預示美國安全風險】網路安全專家批評 Anthropic 與 OpenAI 安全防護措施粗疏，兩家企業的人工智慧模型侵入外部機構系統；專家警示，此類入侵事件預示著日益迫近的國家安全風險。Anthropic 於週四披露，公司利用 Claude 模型開展 141006 次網路安全測評時，本應切斷模型的網際網路訪問許可權。但一處程式漏洞導致該模型在少數場景下接入網際網路，併發起攻擊 —— 模型錯誤認定這些攻擊屬於測試內容。該模型入侵了一家機構，竊取基礎設施訪問憑證以及存有內部生產資料的資料庫。另一起事件中，模型分發惡意軟體，並利用該程式盜取另一家機構的賬號憑證。受影響機構的身份尚未對外公佈。這些入侵事件最早發生在 4 月，但直到上週才被發現。此前 OpenAI 對外披露，自家 AI 智慧體脫離測試環境、侵入開源 AI 模型庫 Hugging Face，Anthropic 隨即對內部網路安全測試專案開展審計，才查出相關問題。英國國家網路安全中心前負責人西亞蘭・馬丁表示：“從網路安全行業視角來看，很多人會認定這屬於防護疏漏。”美國國防部聯合人工智慧中心前戰略與政策主管格雷戈裡・艾倫提出，美軍可以運用先進 AI 模型防護資訊系統，但同時必須認清，這項技術催生了全新類別風險。“正是因為主動排查，Anthropic 才發現了這些入侵行為，” 艾倫表示，“現階段我們根本無從判斷自主 AI 駭客攻擊的普遍程度。”

【美日協同支撐日元 匯市干預或迎來新常態】美國與日本為支撐疲軟日元而展開的協調似乎達到數十年來未有的緊密程度，兩國在日均交易額達9.5萬億美元的全球外匯市場中聯合擊退拋售日元的力量。日本週四可能動用約8.45萬億日元干預匯市，美國當局還向多家銀行詢問日元匯率報價。這種“匯率檢查”通常被視為外匯干預的前兆，也是美國今年第二次採取這一行動。受此影響，日元兌美元在紐約交易時段一度上漲3.3%。在報道稱美國當局通知銀行可能進行支撐日元的交易後，日元在週五進一步走強。截至紐約時間下午2:38，日元兌美元一度上漲0.9%，至158.84。報道稱，紐約聯儲週五要求至少兩家美國大型銀行提供日元兌歐元報價。

### 2026-08-01 03:33:50  #市場 #央行 #國際

【日元走強 紐約聯儲就關鍵匯率向美國大型銀行詢價】隨著市場對日本當局可能再次入市干預的猜測升溫，日元週五上漲。紐約交易時段，日元兌美元和歐元均一度上漲0.9%。兩名知情人士稱，紐約聯儲要求至少兩家美國大型銀行提供日元兌歐元報價。因相關要求非公開，知情人士不願具名。“鑑於日元反應有限，交易員可能會對後續干預行動保持警惕，”道明證券駐新加坡經濟學家Alex Loo表示。週四，日本疑似出手干預匯市後，日元一度大漲3.3%。彭博根據日本央行週五公佈的賬戶資料和貨幣經紀商的預測估算，此次干預規模約為8.45萬億日元（528億美元）。這可能創下日本單日匯市干預規模之最。芝商所資料顯示，週四，大量日元買盤推動其交易量升至近12年來最高水平。但即使干預規模如此之大，對外匯市場的影響仍然有限。日元近幾個月來一度跌至1986年以來最低水平，持續受到油價上漲、財政赤字居高不下以及日美利差懸殊等因素拖累。“歸根結底，除非基本面發生變化，否則匯市干預對匯率的影響可能只是暫時的，”野村國際外匯策略師Yusuke Miyairi表示。

【意法德多國採取措施 應對西班牙飛地移民危機】當地時間7月31日，西班牙內政部表示，自本輪移民危機開始以來，共有5萬名非法移民進入該國飛地休達，其中4.83萬人已自願返回摩洛哥，目前仍有不足2000人滯留當地。此前，休達自治市政府方面援引當地國民警衛隊資料稱，實際非法入境人數約為6萬人，高於西班牙內政部公佈的資料。西班牙首相桑切斯31日在休達表示，此次大規模非法越境是“對西班牙領土完整的侵犯”。截至目前，此次移民危機已造成至少57人死亡，均為在試圖從摩洛哥游泳進入休達的過程中溺亡。當地時間7月31日，義大利宣佈恢復與西班牙之間港口和機場的邊境檢查，以應對西班牙在北非的飛地休達移民危機帶來的影響。義大利政府表示，相關臨時邊境管控措施僅適用於非歐盟公民。義大利政府當天還宣佈，鑑於大批非法移民從摩洛哥湧入休達，義大利暫停與西班牙之間的歐盟“申根”免邊境檢查協議。義大利與西班牙並無陸路接壤，但該措施將影響兩國間的航空及海運旅客往來。義大利內政部在一份宣告中表示，已與法國達成共識，將同步加強法意陸地邊境的管控力度，進一步防範非法移民跨境流動。依據《申根協定》，申根區國家之間不設常規邊檢，人員可自由往來。31日，法國總統馬克龍表示，針對休達近期出現的非法移民湧入事件，法國將與西班牙保持密切協調，並願提供一切必要援助，包括透過歐盟邊境與海岸警衛局提供支援。馬克龍表示，法國和西班牙兩國內政部長正保持聯絡，密切跟蹤局勢。根據他的要求，法國已在法西邊境部署4個機動力量單元，並投入飛機和無人機加強邊境檢查。他強調，休達並不享有前往申根區其他地區的自由流動待遇。（央視新聞）

### 2026-08-01 03:21:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Colombia Holds Interest Rate at 12.0% — Colombia's central bank kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 12.0% on July 31st. The bank noted that annual inflation rose to 6.1% in June, driven by higher food and regulated prices, while core inflation held steady at 6.0%. Inflation expectations for end-2026 and 2027 increased to 6.6% and 5.0%, respectively, with market-based expectations remaining above 6.0%. The bank also noted that economic activity strengthened in the second quarter, supported by robust services sector growth, and maintained its 2026 GDP growth forecast at 2.5%. The unemployment rate fell to 8.0% in June. A stronger Colombian peso has helped ease inflationary pressures, but risks from the Middle East conflict and a potential El Niño event could fuel inflation and weigh on growth. Future policy decisions will remain data dependent.

### 2026-08-01 03:16:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Uruguay #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Uruguay Trade Balance Swings to Deficit in June — Uruguay’s trade balance swung to a deficit of $71.7 million in June 2026 from a surplus of $274.8 million a year earlier. Imports surged 35.5% year-on-year to $1.18 billion, driven by a 57% increase in intermediate goods. Purchases of electricity soared 1,286.1%, while imports of oil and distillates jumped 407.7%. Meanwhile, consumer goods imports rose 13.2% and capital goods imports increased 12.7%. Exports fell 3.2% to $1.11 billion, as shipments of electricity, gas, and water plunged 87%. Exports of primary products also declined 14.8%, while manufactured goods exports increased 7.3%.

【英國工黨敲定十月秋季預算，大範圍減稅讓利地方民生】安迪·伯納姆領導的新一屆英國政府確定預算案日期，將於10月底出臺預算方案，推動資金與權力從威斯敏斯特向外分流。新任首相正穩步推行其稅收與支出規劃。財政大臣約翰·希利宣佈秋季預算定於10月28日釋出。他表示，工黨將嚴守財政規則，相關政策旨在為企業和家庭營造未來發展的穩定環境。伯納姆執政頭兩週密集推出多項政策：下調能源賬單增值稅、削減酒吧、俱樂部與現場音樂場館的商業地產稅、設定公交票價上限，並承諾將部分所得稅收入分配給英格蘭各地區市長。基爾·斯塔默執政時期，工黨民調持續疲軟。伯納姆希望扭轉這一局面，但這份形勢嚴峻的秋季預算必須明確新政資金來源。近幾周，受伊朗衝突拖累，全球經濟承壓、通脹升溫，各國財政壓力加劇，英國政府在全球金融市場的融資成本大幅攀升。希利表示，政府正迅速推出各項舉措重振民眾信心，他將在自己就任財政大臣後的首份預算案中抓緊落實相關工作。

【Cui Dongshu (CPCA): 1H lithium‑ion battery demand +53% YoY; storage, exports and commercial vehicles drive growth】Cui Dongshu of the CPCA said total lithium‑ion battery demand in 1H 2026 reached about 1.069 TWh, up 53% YoY, as industry growth shifted from NEV‑only to a multi‑pillar mix of energy storage, overseas exports and commercial vehicles. New energy vehicle demand totaled ~793.2 GWh, +37% YoY, but domestic NEV passenger retail fell 2% YoY, indicating a move into stock competition; vehicle and battery exports are the primary incremental sources. Battery customs exports grew 54% YoY. Storage demand, the largest growth engine by production‑sales estimates, hit ~275.7 GWh in 1H, up 130% YoY, with June single‑month growth +333% YoY. NEV demand share fell to 74% while storage rose to 26%; storage and overseas markets are now the core supports for industry high cycle.

### 2026-08-01 01:01:32  #公司 #國際

【SpaceX火箭殘骸即將撞月 可為月球撞擊研究提供樣本】一枚約5噸重的SpaceX火箭殘骸即將撞上月球。這枚火箭曾將兩臺月球著陸器送往月球，而本次撞擊並非計劃內操作。 追蹤天體軌道的“冥王星計劃”負責人Bill Gray開發了天體追蹤軟體，據其測算，這枚被遺棄的獵鷹9號火箭上面級，將於美國東部時間8月5日凌晨2點34分左右，以每小時5400英里的速度撞擊月球西側邊緣的愛因斯坦隕石坑附近。 2025年1月15日，SpaceX使用獵鷹9號火箭將螢火蟲航空的“藍幽靈1號”月球著陸器以及日本ispace公司的“韌性號”著陸器一同發射奔赴月球。“藍幽靈1號”於2025年3月2日順利月面軟著陸；而“韌性號”在著陸前90秒失聯，最終墜毀於月球表面。 研究人員介紹，本次任務結束後，獵鷹9號火箭上面級被遺棄在一條會穿越月球軌道的高地球軌道上，如今最終踏上了撞月軌跡。 新墨西哥州洛斯阿拉莫斯國家實驗室博士後研究員Fernando表示：“我們無法確定SpaceX對這次撞擊發生的知情程度。可以確定的是，由於該上面級承擔了月球載荷運送任務，最終被送入的軌道本身就存在撞月可能性。究竟是公司預判到風險卻未對外說明，還是根本沒有做軌道演算，這一點我們無從考證。”

### 2026-08-01 00:42:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Rebound on Choppy Trading — US equities rebounded on a choppy Friday amid more volatility for the AI infrastructure sector as markets considered the impact of higher long-term rates. The S&P 500, Dow, and Nasdaq 100 were around 0.4% up. Amazon surged 15% after beating expectations on its cloud-computing arm. Software and hyperscalers were also higher on reconsidered sentiment in the AI trade, with Alphabet gaining 6% while Microsoft and Meta rose nearly 3%. On the other hand, Apple tanked 10% as the shortage for chips raised costs and lowered production in the June quarter, denting earnings. Meanwhile, broader sectors also softened with Netflix and Eli Lilly losing 3% each. The 30-year Treasury yield surged further to 19-year highs on concerns that Kevin Warsh's FOMC could refrain from raising rates to fight inflation. Also, ExxonMobil sank 3% as limited refinery capacity prevented the oil giant form higher windfalls in oil profits last quarter.

Ibovespa Edges Higher as Market Reopens After Delay — The Ibovespa edged higher to trade above 177,500 on Friday after the market opening was delayed due to operational issues. Major banks also advanced, with Itaú rising more than 1%. Vale (-0.5%) reported 2Q26 earnings and revenue above expectations and raised its 2026 guidance, but posted a 35% decline in profit. Meanwhile, Braskem (-2%) recorded lower sales in 2Q26, pressured by weaker demand for key chemicals. On the macro front, Brazil’s gross public debt-to-GDP ratio rose to 81.9% in June from 81.0% in May. The deterioration in fiscal accounts reinforced expectations that borrowing costs may remain elevated.

【Trump approval falls to post-second-term low】Political analyst Nate Silver said President Trump’s approval rating this month has fallen to a new low in his second term amid the unpopular Iran war and a spike in gasoline prices. "The timing aligns closely with gas prices and the renewed Iran conflict," he said. As the war approaches six months, AAA data show U.S. average gasoline at about $4.11/gal as of Friday morning, versus roughly $3.15/gal a year earlier. Decision Desk HQ poll averages put Trump’s overall approval at 40.6% and disapproval at 57.5% as of Friday morning. A Quinnipiac University poll showed 60% of U.S. voters oppose the war—the highest opposition since the conflict began on Feb. 28—and nearly three quarters oppose sending U.S. troops. An AP-NORC poll found 64% of Americans say the war is not worth it, including 87% of Democrats, 37% of Republicans and 68% of independents.

### 2026-07-31 23:30:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

Canada Budget Deficit Shrinks in May — Canada's federal budget deficit narrowed to CAD 0.31 billion in May 2026 from CAD 2.2 billion in the same month a year earlier. Revenues increased 12.6% year-on-year to CAD 41.94 billion, driven primarily by higher income and excise tax receipts. Meanwhile, program expenses, excluding net actuarial losses, rose 6.2% to CAD 36.68 billion, reflecting higher direct program spending and larger transfers to persons, partly offset by lower transfers to provinces, territories and municipalities. Public debt charges increased 12.7% to CAD 5.16 billion due to higher effective interest rates on a larger stock of marketable bonds. Net actuarial losses rose 23.9% to CAD 415 million, remaining unchanged from the previous month. The budgetary deficit before net actuarial losses was CAD 0.1 billion, compared to a deficit of CAD 1.86 billion in the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-07-31 23:27:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sri Lanka #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Sri Lanka Trade Deficit Widens in June — Sri Lanka's trade deficit widened to $829 million in June 2026 from $540 million a year earlier, as import growth outpaced export gains. The external sector continued to reflect the impact of developments in the Middle East. Imports rose 17.3% year-on-year to $1.97 billion, driven by a 40.2% increase in fuel import expenditure, mainly due to higher spending on refined petroleum products. Meanwhile, exports edged up 0.2% to $1.14 billion. The services account surplus narrowed 33.8% from a year earlier to $162 million, as services outflows increased faster than inflows. In the first half of 2026, the cumulative trade deficit widened to $5.5 billion from $3.3 billion in the corresponding period of 2025.

### 2026-07-31 23:25:15  #公司 #市場 #國際

【埃克森美孚財報電話會議要點總結】
1、財報會議期間股價持續下跌：儘管布倫特原油價格攀升，該公司股價在電話會議期間仍繼續走低，盤中最大跌幅達2.8%。
2、蓋亞那專案引發“困惑”：分析師指出，市場對蓋亞那專案存在一定“困惑”。埃克森美孚目前已收回該專案的成本，這意味著其從該專案中分得的石油產量比例將會降低。對此，首席財務官Neil Hansen反駁稱，由於成本下降，公司理應獲得更多的自由現金流。
3、全球煉油產能嚴重短缺：全球煉油產能面臨極度短缺，導致即使原油價格下跌，燃料價格依然居高不下。執行長Darren Woods表示：“我從未見過可用產能相對於需求的比例像今天這樣低。”
4、批評歐洲的嚴苛政策：Woods猛烈抨擊了歐洲推行嚴苛的甲烷排放法規和暴利稅，認為這可能會阻礙投資並推高消費者的能源價格。他表示：“我希望歐洲民眾終有一天能醒悟過來，認識到他們正在做出多麼糟糕的政策決定。”
5、霍爾木茲海峽或將重新開放：Woods表示，霍爾木茲海峽對全球經濟至關重要，不可能被“永久封鎖”，因此很可能會重新開放。他說：“它一定會恢復通航，因為世界需要它。”

### 2026-07-31 23:21:05  #MKT News #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bearish

【US-Japan coordinated support for yen; intervention may become new normal】US support for Japan’s yen-stabilization is deepening, with coordination at levels not seen in decades. On Thursday Japan reportedly bought yen, estimated at about $53bln, while US authorities made exchange-rate inquiries to banks — moves the market viewed as a precursor to intervention. Treasury Secretary Bessent said the yen is “severely undervalued” and excessive volatility is unhealthy. Nobuyasu Atago, chief economist at Lotte Securities and a former BOJ official, said Bessent’s influence is significant and the US is increasingly cooperating with Japanese intervention. The timing — between Fed and BOJ rate decisions — has spurred market speculation that monetary-policy coordination may be part of the effort. Japanese officials remained ambiguous on further intervention but emphasized US coordination; Jun Mimura, the Ministry of Finance’s top currency official, said the US provided more than “moral support.”

Colombia Unemployment Rate Holds at 8% — Colombia's unemployment rate held steady at 8% in June 2026, unchanged from the previous month and slightly below market expectations of 8.1%. The unemployment rate stood at 9.9% for women and 6.6% for men, resulting in a gender gap of 3.3 percentage points. Across the country's 13 major metropolitan areas, the unemployment rate was 8.3%. The highest jobless rates were recorded in Quibdó (23.3%), Cartagena (13.2%), and Ibagué (12.8%), while Medellín Metropolitan Area (7.1%), Pereira Metropolitan Area (7.2%), and Villavicencio (7.9%) posted the lowest rates. The national employment rate stood at 59.4%. In the April-June 2026 period, the average unemployment rate was 8.3%.

### 2026-07-31 23:01:16  #MKT News #GBPUSD #Impact bearish #FTSE100 #Impact bullish

【Bank of England hawks increasingly isolated; deputy governors shift toward pausing hikes】Bank of England's MPC stance is shifting: hawkish members are becoming isolated. Thursday's 6-3 vote to raise rates—driven by concern over an Iran-related energy shock—masks a broader migration toward dovishness among other MPC members, notably deputy governors Lombard and Ramsden. Both had warned of second-round inflation from higher energy, but those concerns are easing as growth softens and firms curb hiring. Markets now see a majority consensus to keep rates unchanged unless inflation risks re-emerge; Ramsden said if the conflict eases and domestic price pressures continue to fall the Bank may resume its previously paused rate-cut process, while Lombard said current support for a hold is not "close to a tipping point." Analysts say the hawk–dove boundary is being redrawn and the bar for further hikes has risen; rate markets price only one possible hike this year.

### 2026-07-31 22:55:20  #公司

【英國石油公司將出售北海油氣業務】英國石油公司7月31日說，將出售其位於北海的石油和天然氣業務，結束在該區域60餘年的經營活動。行業人士分析，此舉與近年來英國能源政策變動頻繁、北海油氣產量下降等因素有關。據路透社報道，英國石油公司新任執行長梅格·奧尼爾在宣告中說，公司計劃將資金集中投入開發“價值最高的機遇”，因此決定出售北海油氣業務。英國石油公司已在北海經營油氣業務逾60年，目前在當地運營5個主要生產中心，包括英國大陸架規模最大的克萊爾油田。該公司2025年約5%的油氣產自北海，日產量約為11.7萬桶石油當量。就在英國石油公司宣佈出售北海業務的前一天，英國新任首相伯納姆表示，計劃以“務實”態度開發和利用北海油氣資源。總部位於倫敦的跨國交易平臺IG集團首席市場分析師克里斯·比徹姆對此解讀說：“顯然，英國石油公司認為在亟須開發新油氣田的當下，觀望新一屆政府能否重振英國能源政策太過耗時，而坐等政府採取行動並非審慎的資源利用方式。”據路透社報道，英國油氣行業人士認為，英國近年來多次調整針對油氣行業的稅收制度，這種政策不確定性損害了企業對投資英國大陸架油氣資源的信心。北海油氣田產量已從本世紀初的日均450萬桶石油當量，大幅降至2025年的日均約100萬桶石油當量。近年來，埃克森美孚、雪佛龍、康菲、殼牌、道達爾、埃尼等跨國能源巨頭紛紛縮減了在北海的油氣業務。（新華社）

Silver Posts Monthly Loss as Rate Hike Expectations Weigh — Silver fell more than 2% to around $57.5 an ounce on Friday, ending July down over 1% as the US dollar rebounded from a more than one-month low and expectations for tighter Federal Reserve policy outweighed support from softer US inflation data and unchanged interest rates. Markets currently see about a 65% chance of a Fed rate hike in September, with another increase expected by June 2027. Chicago Business Barometer Rises in July — The Chicago Business Barometer rose to 57.6 in July 2026 from 56.7 in the previous month, remaining in expansionary territory for a third straight month and topping market expectations of 56.0. The increase was driven by new orders, which climbed to their highest level since January 2022. Meanwhile, supplier deliveries weakened, while order backlogs slipped back into contraction after two months above the neutral 50 mark. Employment remained in contraction for a fifth consecutive month, falling to its lowest level since March, while production eased but stayed in expansion. Prices paid was little changed, as respondents continued to cite geopolitical tensions and elevated energy costs despite signs of improving price stability.

Brent Set for Strong Monthly Advance — Brent crude oil climbed above $88 a barrel on Friday, extending its monthly advance to more than 20% amid renewed hostilities between the US and Iran and reports that some tankers were forced to turn around in the Strait of Hormuz. According to Iran’s Fars news agency, the Revolutionary Guards stopped two tankers from passing through the waterway, while four others altered course. ### 2026-07-31 22:29:30  #宏觀

【三部門負責人就《國務院關於出境入境管理的規定》答記者問】問：在規範我國公民出境管理方面作了哪些規定？

答：近年來，隨著我國公民出境人數不斷增長，實踐中出現了上當受騙出境或者虛構事由非法出境從事跨境賭博、電信網路詐騙等違法活動，嚴重危害人民群眾生命和財產安全，或者違規出境後向境外非法轉移技術，危害國家產業安全、技術安全等問題。

針對上述問題，規定一方面加強出境管理，要求出境人員申請出境的事由應當真實、合法，並配合移民管理機構核實身份、申請事由；規定出具邀請函件的單位和個人應當對邀請內容的真實性負責；對提供虛假材料、作出虛假陳述的，移民管理機構有權決定不予簽發出境入境證件或者不準其出境。

另一方面，規定根據出境入境管理法的授權，完善不準出境情形，對因騙取出境入境證件、非法出境入境受到行政拘留處罰的，在境外從事違法犯罪活動危害國家安全和利益的，以及違反出口管制、技術進出口管理等規定可能危害國家產業安全、技術安全的，依法不準其出境。

### 2026-07-31 22:29:05  #宏觀

【三部門負責人就《國務院關於出境入境管理的規定》答記者問】問：在規範外國人入境管理方面作了哪些規定？

答：為進一步規範外國人入境管理，規定主要作了以下規定：一是加強入境管理。要求入境人員申請入境、停留居留的事由應當真實、合法，並配合移民管理機構、簽證機關核實身份、申請事由；規定出具邀請函件的單位和個人應當對邀請內容的真實性負責。二是完善不準入境情形。根據出境入境管理法的授權，明確外國人在境外申請辦理中國簽證或者在口岸申請入境時提供虛假材料、作出虛假陳述的，因妨害國（邊）境管理受到刑事處罰，或者因騙取出境入境證件、非法出境入境受到行政處罰的，依法不準其入境。三是強化入境反制措施實施。為加強反制裁、反干預、反“長臂管轄”法治應對，規定外國人被列入反制清單、不可靠實體清單、惡意實體清單或者被採取反制和限制措施等，需要依法採取不予簽發出境入境證件或者不準入境等相關措施的，由移民管理機構、簽證機關按照職責實施。

In a Friday statement, Hamak and Kashkari said they worry that while recent price gains may partly reflect President Trump’s tariffs and the Iran conflict, inflation now warrants Fed action. Logan joined, saying even if inflation cools it is unlikely to return fully to the Fed’s 2% target without tighter policy; absent policy restraint inflation could remain above target until an external shock. Kashkari said he may support a series of rate hikes rather than a single increase to prevent inflation from becoming entrenched. Hamak warned that without policy tightening price growth could accelerate and said she lacks confidence inflation will revert to 2% on its own after running above target for more than five years.

### 2026-07-31 22:28:29  #宏觀

【三部門負責人就《國務院關於出境入境管理的規定》答記者問】問：在防範我國公民出境安全風險、保護公民人身安全方面作了哪些規定？

答：為了更加有效地防範我國公民出境後的安全風險，保護公民人身安全，規定堅持預防為主、綜合施策的原則，主要作了以下規定。

一方面，及時釋出境外安全風險提醒提示。要求國務院外交、文化和旅遊主管部門以及駐外外交機構根據有關國家和地區戰爭或者武裝衝突、社會治安、自然災害、事故災難、傳染病疫情等安全形勢，及時公開發布國外安全提醒和旅遊目的地安全風險提示。

另一方面，完善出境環節安全風險防範制度。要求移民管理機構在受理、審批出境入境證件申請和實施出境邊防檢查時，提醒公民謹慎前往高風險國家或者地區，對確需前往的提醒其關注當地安全形勢，加強安全防範，必要時勸阻其前往風險等級為最高階別或者嚴重危及人身安全案件突發高發的國家或者地區。

【美國消費者信心升至五個月高位 對經濟前景看法改善】由於消費者對前景的看法改善且汽油價格維持在戰爭期間高點以下，美國7月消費者信心升至五個月高點。密歇根大學週五公佈的調查顯示，7月消費者信心指數終值升至55.2。這高於初值的54.4。此次調查於6月23日至7月27日期間進行。在此期間，汽油價格一度跌至3月以來最低水平，隨後中東局勢再度緊張，汽油價格再次上漲。“消費者仍主要關注購買力等切身經濟問題，而政治或軍事方面的動向仍處於次要地位，”調查負責人Joanne Hsu在宣告中表示。“儘管近期信心有所改善，但消費者信心仍較一年前低11%，反映出他們對經濟整體仍持較為悲觀的看法。”儘管高物價仍令居民承壓，但本週稍早公佈的資料顯示，消費者支出保持韌性。週五公佈的報告還顯示，衡量耐用品購買景氣度的指標升至去年10月以來最高水平。現狀指標和預期指標均較一個月前改善。不同收入、年齡、教育程度及政治立場的受訪者信心均有所提升。對長期經濟形勢的看法升至一年來最高。消費者預計未來一年物價將以4.2%的幅度上漲，低於6月。他們還預計，未來5至10年物價將以3.3%的年率上漲，與6月持平。報告指出，人工智慧已成為消費者關注的“突出”因素。Hsu表示，受訪者對AI的評價整體偏負面，不過也有人提到，AI有助於提高生產率，但可能對就業市場產生負面影響。

German Bund Yields Surge as ECB Rate Hike Bets Intensify — Germany's 10-year Bund yield climbed toward 3.2%, approaching the 15-year high reached last week and posting a gain of more than 30 basis points in July, its largest monthly increase since March. Annual inflation also accelerated to 2.9% in July, while core and services inflation strengthened. In the US, the Federal Reserve left interest rates unchanged this week, with markets expecting two rate hikes by June next year.

Meanwhile, Fairfax Financial (-2%) and Cameco (-0.1%) reported revenue above expectations, but both missed EPS forecasts. Enbridge slipped more than 0.5% despite beating estimates for second-quarter adjusted profit, while Imperial Oil fell 2% despite reporting stronger-than-expected quarterly earnings. In contrast, financials advanced, with RBC, TD Bank, and BMO adding about 0.5% each. Meanwhile, a first estimate showed the Canadian economy grew 0.8% in the second quarter, the most in nearly two years, to show some resiliency to inflatonary pressure.

### 2026-07-31 22:08:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Michigan Inflation Expectations #Importance 1

US 1-Year Inflation Expectations Confirmed at 4.2% — Year-ahead inflation expectations in the United States, as measured by the University of Michigan survey, fell for a second consecutive month to 4.2% in July 2026, the lowest level since March, down from 4.6% in June and in line with the preliminary estimate. Even so, expectations remained well above the 3.4% recorded in February, before the outbreak of the Iran conflict, and above all readings seen throughout 2024. Meanwhile, the five-year inflation outlook stood at 3.3% in July, matching both June's reading and the preliminary estimate. Nevertheless, it remained slightly above the 2.8%-3.2% range that prevailed throughout 2024.

---

### 2026-07-31 22:07:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Consumer Confidence #Importance 2

US Consumer Sentiment Revised Higher to Five-Month High — The University of Michigan's Consumer Sentiment Index was revised higher to 55.2 in July 2026 from a preliminary reading of 54.0, reaching its highest level since February, before the US-Iran conflict drove gas prices higher. Sentiment improved across all income, age, education, wealth, and political groups. Consumers' five-year outlook for business conditions climbed to a 12-month high, though it remained well below its historical average. Despite the improvement, overall sentiment was still 11% lower than a year earlier, reflecting continued concerns over elevated prices and the lasting effects of several years of high inflation. Year-ahead inflation expectations eased to 4.2% from 4.6% in June, while long-run expectations held steady at 3.3%. Interviews for the survey were conducted between June 23 and July 27.

### 2026-07-31 22:03:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Currency #Importance 0

Euro Ends Month Steady as Strong Data Counters Geopolitical Tensions — The euro traded around $1.145, ending the month little changed against the US dollar as stronger-than-expected eurozone data offset mounting geopolitical tensions. The eurozone economy grew 0.4% in the second quarter, beating forecasts of 0.2% and marking its strongest expansion since early 2025. Meanwhile, annual inflation accelerated to 2.9% in July, with core and services inflation also strengthening. The data reinforced expectations for further ECB tightening, with markets fully pricing in the deposit rate reaching 2.75% by early 2027, implying two additional rate hikes, the first potentially in September. In the US, the Federal Reserve kept interest rates unchanged, although three FOMC members dissented in favor of a rate hike. Markets now assign about a 65% probability of a September increase. Meanwhile, oil prices jumped more than 20% this month after renewed US-Iran strikes, fueling concerns over energy supplies and inflation.

### 2026-07-31 21:53:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

US 10 Year Treasury Yield Back to 2025-Highs — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note rose to 4.73% on Friday, returning to its highest level since January 2025, as investors weighed remarks from Fed policymakers that reinforced the case for higher interest rates to curb inflation. Minneapolis Fed President Kashkari said he would prefer smaller rate hikes now rather than waiting, while Cleveland Fed President Hammack warned that the longer inflation remains elevated, the more difficult and costly it will be to bring it back to target. Dallas Fed President Logan said inflation risks are to the upside. These are the three FOMC members who dissented at this week's meeting, voting in favor of a 25bps rate hike, although the Fed left the federal funds rate unchanged. Even so, investors' expectations for a rate hike at the next policy meeting in September eased after Governor Warsh offered little guidance on the future path of monetary policy, although markets continue to price in roughly a two-thirds probability of a 25bps increase.

【蘋果股價創2025年以來最大跌幅 供應短缺拖累業績展望】蘋果股價創16個月來最大跌幅，零部件短缺拖累了這家科技巨頭的銷售預期，這表明全行業面臨的供應鏈限制所造成的負面影響比預期更為嚴重。蘋果週四在財報電話會上表示，截至9月的第四財季營收預計將增長9%至11%。分析師此前預計增幅將超過12%。蘋果一直受到儲存晶片和電腦處理器短缺的困擾，這迫使公司6月上調了Mac和iPad價格。供應緊張導致Mac mini和Mac Studio等重要電腦產品的交貨等待期延長。首席財務官Kevan Parekh在電話會上表示，供應限制將在截至9月的季度影響iPhone、Mac和iPad業務。他說，匯率波動也在拖累增長。令人失望的業績展望導致蘋果股價週五開盤一度大跌9.5%，至301.83美元，創2025年4月3日以來最大盤中跌幅。某種程度上，上財季也是執行長蒂姆·庫克的謝幕之作。庫克將於9月1日把管理權交給硬體業務負責人約翰·特努斯。自2011年執掌蘋果以來，庫克推動了產品線多元化，並將公司年度銷售額提高到接近5000億美元。

US Stocks Rebound on Friday — Futures tracking US equity indices extended yesterday's rebound amid another strong session for chip producers and optimistic corporate results. Contract for the S&P 500 and the Dow were around 0.5% higher, while the Nasdaq 100 rose over 1%. Amazon surged over 10% premarket after reporting an acceleration in cloud-computing revenue for a fifth quarter, easing concerns that soaring AI spending would not produce returns. The rhetoric was aligned with a second session of gains for chip producers as markets grappled with concerns that spending could slow on AI infrastructure against the large potential upside on the trade. Micron and Sandisk gained, erasing the selloff from the week, while AMD added 4% and Nvidia gained 3%. In turn, Apple slipped 7% as the shortage for chips raised costs and dented production. Meanwhile, Exxon and Chevron were mixed after their earnings, unveiling the magnitude of the benefit from soaring energy costs from the Middle East-war.

### 2026-07-31 21:21:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Copper Production #Importance 1

Chile Copper Output Rebounds in June — Chile's copper production rose 5.1% year-on-year to 447,294 metric tons in June 2026, rebounding from a 12.9% decline in May. The increase was driven by higher ore processing at the country's major mining companies. The recovery in Chile, the world's largest copper producer, helped ease concerns over tightening global copper supply. Meanwhile, Chile's Mining Production Index rose 6% from a year earlier, supported by a 4.6% increase in metallic mining, a 14.5% rise in non-metallic mining, and a 4.3% gain in energy resources production.

### 2026-07-31 21:20:31  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美國第二季度僱傭成本穩步增長 勞動力成本壓力依舊溫和】美國第二季度勞動力成本保持平穩增長，顯示就業市場對通脹壓力的推動作用依然有限。美國勞工統計局週五公佈的資料顯示，僱傭成本指數同比上漲3.4%。該指數衡量工資和福利成本的變化。不過，經通脹因素調整後，勞動報酬成本實際有所下降。按季度計算，僱傭成本指數環比上漲0.9%。美聯儲一直密切關注勞動力市場狀況。雖然第二季度初招聘活動較為強勁，但到季度末已逐漸放緩。週五公佈的資料表明，季度初招聘回暖並未明顯推高薪資增速，顯示勞動力市場整體仍大致處於供需平衡狀態。美聯儲主席凱文·沃什週三在宣佈維持利率不變後表示，“就業增長與勞動力規模基本保持同步，失業率變化不大。”另一份週四公佈的資料顯示，美國6月通脹有所降溫，但仍明顯高於美聯儲2%的目標。週五公佈的僱傭成本指數報告還顯示，截至6月的三個月，美國民間勞動者工資和薪金環比增長0.9%，同比增長3.2%。不過，經通脹調整後，工資和薪金同比實際下降。

【Zhongchuang Xinhang battery enters Xiaomi Pengcheng supply chain; 177Ah quality dispute unresolved】Xiaomi priced Pengcheng N90 Max and N70 Max at 299,900 yuan and 259,900 yuan on July 30, marking its entry into the range‑extended segment. ### 2026-07-31 21:14:45  #其他

【中央教育工作領導小組秘書組、中共教育部黨組印發通知，部署學習貫徹習近平總書記對基礎教育工作重要指示精神】 
近日，中央教育工作領導小組秘書組、中共教育部黨組印發《關於深入學習貫徹習近平總書記對基礎教育工作重要指示精神的通知》，就學習宣傳貫徹落實有關工作作出部署。

《通知》指出，在全國基礎教育工作會議召開之際，習近平總書記對基礎教育工作作出重要指示，深刻闡明新時代基礎教育工作的戰略地位和取得的歷史性成就，把立德樹人作為教育根本任務，把健康第一作為重要教育理念，堅持辦好人民滿意教育的根本立場，把教師隊伍建設作為基礎工作，要求切實扛起政治責任，不斷開創基礎教育高質量發展新局面。習近平總書記的重要指示，充分體現了黨中央對基礎教育的高度重視，對廣大青少年兒童健康成長的殷切期待，為新徵程上深入推進基礎教育高質量發展提供了行動綱領和科學指南。

《通知》強調，學習貫徹習近平總書記重要指示精神是當前和今後一個時期教育戰線的重大政治任務，要準確把握重點任務和工作要求，全力推動習近平總書記重要指示精神落地見效。全面貫徹落實黨的教育方針，教育引導廣大學生從小堅定理想信念、厚植家國情懷，加強體育鍛煉、美育薰陶和勞動實踐，促進學生健康成長、全面發展。牢牢守住教育公平底線，深入實施縣中振興行動計劃，推進中小學陽光招生，進一步縮小城鄉、區域、校際、群體教育差距。適應人口變化最佳化教育資源配置，加強資源整體統籌，促進校際間、學段間資源高效利用。守正創新塑造基礎教育新優勢，堅持以學生成長為中心深化教育教學改革，注重科技教育和人文教育協同。進一步強化協同共育責任，凝聚各方育人合力。加強高素質專業化教師隊伍建設，大力弘揚教育家精神，堅持師德師風第一標準，打造高質量教師教育體系，加快提升教師專業素養。

《通知》要求，各地要迅速興起學習宣傳貫徹習近平總書記重要指示精神的熱潮，開展學習研討，強化宣傳引導，深化研究實踐，學深悟透、融會貫通，持續將習近平總書記重要指示精神學習宣傳貫徹工作引向深入。（教育部）

### 2026-07-31 21:11:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Chile Industrial Production Rebounds in June — Chile's industrial production rose 1.3% year-on-year in June 2026, rebounding from a 7.5% decline in May and marking the first expansion in nine months. The recovery was driven by a 6% increase in mining output, as metallic mining rose 4.6%, non-metallic mining advanced 14.5%, and energy resources production increased 4.3%. Copper production was up 5.1% from a year earlier. Utilities output also grew 2.4%, led by a 3.5% increase in electricity generation, supported by higher output from coal and coal-petcoke power plants. In contrast, manufacturing output contracted 3.2%, mainly due to an 8.1% decline in food production. On a monthly basis, industrial production increased 1.6%.

### 2026-07-31 21:10:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Chile Jobless Rate Steady at 2021-Highs — Chile's unemployment rate stood at 9.4% in the April-June 2026 period, unchanged from the previous rolling quarter and in line with market expectations. The reading remained at its highest level since the quarter ending June 2021. The labor force increased 1.5% year-on-year, while the labor force participation rate edged down to 62.3% from 62.4%, as the number of unemployed eased slightly to 980 thousand. Meanwhile, employment rose 0.9% from a year earlier to 9.403 million, although the employment rate edged down to 56.4% from 56.5% in the previous period. Job creation was primarily driven by gains in transportation (6.8%), administrative and support service activities (12.9%), and human health activities (5.1%).

【非盟會議警告“超級厄爾尼諾”將使非洲面臨嚴峻挑戰】非洲聯盟農業、農村發展、藍色經濟和可持續環境委員摩西·維拉卡蒂30日在衣索比亞首都亞的斯亞貝巴表示，今年晚些時候可能出現數十年來最強厄爾尼諾事件，非洲大陸許多地區將面臨極端天氣和災害風險。他呼籲非洲各國加強科學預警，採取切實有效的防災措施，保護生命、生計和經濟發展。 維拉卡蒂當天在非盟總部舉行的應對厄爾尼諾部長級特別磋商會議上說，科學預測表明，今年晚些時候發生“超級厄爾尼諾事件”的機率高達90%，預計強度峰值將出現在2026年11月至2027年2月之間。“這將是自1950年以來最強的一次（厄爾尼諾事件）。這並非遙遠的可能事件，而是迫在眉睫的風險，我們需要在國家、區域和大陸層面立即做好準備。” 維拉卡蒂指出，根據預測，非洲大陸許多地區可能面臨嚴峻挑戰，包括嚴重乾旱、毀滅性洪水、持續熱浪、糧食生產中斷、水資源短缺、疾病暴發、人道主義需求增加等。“這些影響不僅會波及弱勢家庭，還會給各國經濟、公共財政和區域穩定造成巨大壓力。” “歷史表明，及時採取行動能夠挽救生命、保護經濟並維護發展成果。”維拉卡蒂說，非盟隨時準備與成員國、專業機構和發展夥伴等攜手合作，加快落實各項措施，調動資源，支援整個非洲大陸的防災減災工作。 (新華社)

【國務院國資委：深化央企“人工智慧+”專項行動 因企制宜培育新興產業和未來產業】7月31日，國務院國資委黨委召開擴大會議。會議強調，國資央企要堅決把思想和行動統一到黨中央對當前經濟形勢的科學判斷和經濟工作部署上來，清醒把握當前形勢，進一步堅定信心決心，用好各種機遇和優勢，全力以赴提質增效穩增長，堅決完成全年目標任務，推動中央企業高質量發展行穩致遠。要指導中央企業主動對接和帶頭落實好國家政策，抓住重點、主動作為，充分發揮引領帶動作用，更好助推經濟持續向新向優向好發展。要錨定全年目標不動搖，切實加強現場督導調研，把中央企業生產經營情況摸清摸透，找準痛點、對症下藥，夯實企業高質量發展基礎。要著力推進原始創新和關鍵核心技術攻關，以更大力度加強基礎研究，深化央企“人工智慧+”專項行動，因企制宜培育新興產業和未來產業。要持續深化提質增效，做好重點領域內卷治理，推進全產業鏈、全價值鏈降本節支，增強經營創現能力和資本積累積聚能力，以“自身穩”帶動“行業穩”。要堅決守住風險底線，有效發揮司庫體系功能作用，切實加強穿透監管，紮實抓好防汛救災和安全生產工作，築牢高質量發展的安全屏障。

### 2026-07-31 20:54:58  #宏觀 #公司

【上半年我國保險業原保險保費收入3.86萬億元】中國保險行業協會7月31日釋出的資料顯示，2026年上半年，保險市場執行總體平穩，業務結構持續最佳化，原保險保費收入3.86萬億元，同比增長3.3%；賠款與給付支出1.4萬億元，同比增長3.8%。 上半年，財產險公司實現原保險保費收入9846億元，同比增長2.1%；人身險公司實現原保險保費收入約2.87萬億元，同比增長3.6%；外資保險公司市場份額上升至9.7%。 中國保險行業協會副秘書長方詠在當日召開的2026年度第二次例行新聞釋出會上介紹，目前，針對廣西、湖北、甘肅等20個省份暴雨洪澇、颱風、地震等自然災害，保險業累計接報案53.3萬件，估損110.5億元，已賠付55.3億元，其中對暴雨洪澇及颱風災害賠付52.1億元。 方詠表示，災前，各地保險行業協會出臺車險災害互認指引，保險機構開展風險排查、應急演練，前置儲備救援物資與跨區支援力量；災中，啟動省市兩級應急響應，依託警保、政保聯動推行無差別免費施救；災後，保險業落地“三免四快”便民服務，全力支援災後重建。目前理賠工作有序推進，保險業將持續跟蹤災情，助力受災地區儘快恢復正常生產生活秩序。(新華社)

### 2026-07-31 20:44:09  #其他

【皇崗口岸港方口岸區正式成立】香港特區政府新聞處31日表示，皇崗口岸港方口岸區已於當日零時正式成立，由香港特區依照本地法律實施管轄。 據瞭解，位於深圳的皇崗口岸重建後將實施“一地兩檢”安排，定位為港深之間最重要的24小時客運陸路口岸。《皇崗口岸港方口岸區條例》31日生效，該條例為特區政府在皇崗口岸設立港方口岸區，以及在港方口岸區依法實施管轄提供法律基礎。 為配合港方口岸區作為禁區的管理、指定羈留設施、交通及運輸管理的配套安排，以及提供公共流動無線電通訊服務等事宜，特區政府還制定了6項附屬法例。該6項附屬法例與條例同步於31日生效。 香港特區政府保安局局長鄧炳強31日前往皇崗口岸港方口岸區視察，聽取各部門彙報設施準備情況，並主持跨部門工作小組會議，全力推進口岸開放前的各項籌備工作。 特區政府發言人表示，特區政府將繼續與深圳方面保持緊密合作，有序推進系統測試、運作及應急演練，確保口岸設施及安排完善。待雙方確認具備安全、暢順運作的條件後，粵港兩地政府將敲定正式開通日期，並適時向公眾公佈。(新華社)

【Oxford Economics: rising inflation risks boost odds of ECB September rate hike】Oxford Economics analyst Iain Simmons says euro‑area inflation remains above target and faces material upside risks. Middle East tensions have pushed energy prices higher; July headline inflation rose 0.1ppt to 2.9%. Since the breakdown of the Iran ceasefire, the risk of broader inflation spillovers has increased, strengthening the case for an ECB rate rise in September. Simmons adds that yesterday’s stronger‑than‑expected GDP print suggests the euro‑area may better tolerate tightening, but inflation’s response to Middle East developments has so far been muted and any second‑round wage effects later this year may be limited.

### 2026-07-31 20:37:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Monthly GDP MoM #Importance 2

Canada GDP Expands Further — The Canadian GDP is expected to have grown by 0.2% from the previous month in June of 2026, translating to a 0.8% expansion in the second quarter. The preliminary data indicated higher output for wholesale, finance and insurance, and retail trade. These offset declines for utilities and agriculture. Richer data showed that the GDP expanded by 0.3% in May, reflecting some resilience despite the inflationary pressure from higher energy prices in the period due to the war in Iran. Goods producing industries expanded by 0.6% amid as higher oil and gas prices lifted mining, quarrying, and oil and gas extraction output by 1%. Meanwhile, services expanded by 0.2%, as a 0.4% growth rate in real estate and 0.3% jump in retail trade offset a 0.4% contraction in wholesale trade.

【胡錫進:建議查一查今天誰在砸盤】胡錫進發文稱，今天A股的表現實在令人失望，美股科技股昨夜猛烈反彈，韓國股市今天股指漲了17.91％，A股經過前邊的調整，又趕上外部大漲，應當說今天A股科技股強烈反彈的條件非常充分。所以今天一開盤多隻科技股漲停，創業板漲到10％以上的有很多隻，衝擊20cm的希望很大。但是隨後科技股越來越弱，到了收盤時，漲幅大約比開盤時降了一半。不能不說，這:樣的反彈遠低預期，對股市的信心沒有起到鼓舞作用，反而讓唱衰中國股市“就是不行”的那些聲音有了新噱頭。老胡建議有關部門查一查今天是誰在砸盤，誰在對股市信心的恢復釜底抽薪?如果有公募基金那樣做，或者有國資那樣做，應當嚴厲批評警告。中國的股市信心正處在一個敏感的十字路。口，氣可以鼓，決不能洩，證監會等為提:振股市信心這段時間做了很多努力，也很得人心，切不能讓那些努力付之東流。股市繁榮無疑會帶動市場繁榮，振興股市是股民們的期待，也是有利於國家綜合發展的大好事。(胡錫進百家號)

【Fed dissenters say rate hikes may be needed to curb sticky inf】Two Fed officials voted against holding rates unchanged this week, warning delayed action on inf could require more aggressive tightening later. The Cleveland Fed president said the longer high inf persists, the harder and costlier it will be to bring down. Minneapolis Fed president Neel Kashkari said he leans toward gradual tightening while awaiting further inf and jobs data, and argued Fed tools can address supply-driven inf as in the late 1970s–early 1980s. Both said multiple supply shocks are lifting inf; the Cleveland president also flagged demand-side pressure. Both noted the economy remains strong and unemployment is low.

### 2026-07-31 20:14:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

South Africa Trade Surplus Widens in June — South Africa's trade surplus widened to ZAR 17.75 billion in June 2026 from an upwardly revised ZAR 4.44 billion in the previous month, as exports increased while imports declined. Exports rose 4.3% to ZAR 193.6 billion, driven by higher shipments of vehicles and transport equipment (16%), chemical products (6%), prepared foodstuffs (6%), and precious metals and stones (2%). Meanwhile, imports fell 2.9% to ZAR 175.8 billion, reflecting lower purchases of mineral products (-15%), vehicles and transport equipment (-12%), and original equipment components (-6%). Despite the monthly improvement, the trade surplus was smaller than the ZAR 20.96 billion surplus recorded in the same month a year earlier.

【White House urges pause; Fed hawks warn inflation could be entrenched】White House NEC Director Hassett said policy rates can remain on hold, but at the July FOMC meeting Hamak and Kashkari argued for a 25bp hike, moving the debate from timing to how to judge the nature of inflation. Hassett emphasized growth and investment, saying current data do not justify a hike; the hawks warned that successive supply shocks—energy, tariffs—plus AI-driven data‑center investment could convert short-term shocks into sustained price‑setting and inflation expectations. If the hawkish bloc widens, market expectations for rate cuts could be pushed out; persistent White House opposition to hikes would increase scrutiny of Fed independence and its anti-inflation resolve.

### 2026-07-31 20:11:12  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 2

Brazil Producer Prices Fall the Most in a Year — Brazil's producer prices fell 0.77% month-over-month in June 2026, following a 0.30% decline in May and marking the sharpest monthly drop in a year. The decrease was driven mainly by the extractive industries, where producer prices plunged 11.85% as crude oil, iron ore, and natural gas prices declined after geopolitical tensions in the Middle East eased and shipping through the Strait of Hormuz increased. Other major negative contributors included chemicals (-2.74%) and oil refining and biofuels (-2.30%). Among major economic categories, intermediate goods fell 1.7% and were the main drag on the index, while capital goods rose 1.02% and consumer goods increased 0.25%. Producer prices rose 3.99% in the first half of 2026 and were up 2.51% from a year earlier.

### 2026-07-31 19:56:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

Brazil Budget Deficit Widens More Than Expected — The Brazilian government posted a nominal budget deficit of BRL 166 billion in June 2026, widening from BRL 108.1 billion in the same month a year earlier and exceeding market expectations of a BRL 133.2 billion shortfall. The federal government recorded a BRL 47.2 billion deficit, while regional governments posted a BRL 6.6 billion deficit and state-owned companies registered a BRL 5.5 billion shortfall. Meanwhile, gross government debt rose to 81.9% of GDP in July, the highest level since October 2021, up from 81.1% in the previous month and above market forecasts of 81.5%. Over the 12 months through July, the nominal public sector deficit widened to 1.19% of GDP from 1.14% in the previous month.

US Futures Extend Rebound — Futures tracking US equity indices extended yesterday's rebound amid another strong session for chip producers and optimistic corporate results. Contract for the S&P 500 and the Dow were around 0.5% higher, while the Nasdaq 100 rose over 1%. Amazon surged over 10% premarket after reporting an acceleration in cloud-computing revenue for a fifth quarter, easing concerns that soaring AI spending would not produce returns. The rhetoric was aligned with a second session of gains for chip producers as markets grappled with concerns that spending could slow on AI infrastructure against the large potential upside on the trade. Micron and Sandisk were each 5% higher, set to erase the selloff from earlier in the week. In turn, Apple slipped 7% as the shortage for chips raised costs and dented production. Meanwhile, Exxon and Chevron were mixed after their earnings, unveiling the magnitude of the benefit from soaring energy costs from the Middle East-war.

### 2026-07-31 19:32:39  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution

【EU begins enforcing AI Act from Aug. 2; new transparency duties take effect】The European Commission said July 31 that from Aug. 2 the EU AI Office, working with member states' competent authorities, will begin enforcing provisions of the AI Act. New transparency obligations require certain AI systems to notify users when they are interacting with AI and whether content has been generated or modified by AI. Interactive systems such as chatbots must explicitly identify themselves as AI, not a human. Deepfake images, video and audio created or edited by AI must be labeled, and AI-generated/modified content must include machine-readable markers to enable identification and tracing. The Commission said the measures are intended to reduce deception and manipulation and provide clearer compliance guidance for firms.

【歐盟8月2日起執行《人工智慧法》相關規定 新增AI透明度要求】歐盟委員會7月31日宣佈，自8月2日起，歐盟委員會人工智慧辦公室將與各成員國主管部門共同開始執行《人工智慧法》相關規定。同時，新的人工智慧透明度要求也將正式生效，要求部分人工智慧系統明確告知使用者其正在與人工智慧互動，以及相關內容是否由人工智慧生成或修改。根據新規，聊天機器人等互動式人工智慧系統必須向使用者明確表明其人工智慧身份，而非真人。利用人工智慧生成或編輯的深度偽造圖片、影片和音訊等內容必須進行標識，由人工智慧生成或修改的內容還需新增機器可識別標記，以便識別和追蹤。歐盟委員會表示，這些措施旨在減少欺騙和操縱行為，幫助公眾作出更加知情的判斷，同時為企業提供更加明確的合規要求和實施路徑。歐盟委員會當天還公佈了首批簽署《人工智慧生成內容透明度行為準則》的180多家機構名單。該準則旨在推動落實《人工智慧法》關於人工智慧生成內容透明度的相關規定。（央視新聞）

【亞太經合組織第67次能效與節能專家組會議在深圳召開】7月30日，亞太經合組織（APEC）第67次能效與節能專家組會議在深圳召開。本次會議以“提升系統級能效：亞太經合組織政策協調與創新區域能源服務”為主題，參會代表圍繞亞太經合組織能源工作組框架下的重點任務與目標進展、系統級能效政策協同、區域能源服務創新模式、重點領域節能降碳最佳實踐等議題深入交流研討。能效與節能專家組是亞太經合組織能源工作組下設4個專家組之一，目前由中國標準化研究院專家擔任主席。本次會議是2026APEC中國年能源領域系列會議活動之一，亞太能源研究中心同時配套舉辦了“能效與節能能力建設”APEC基金專案研討會。來自亞太經合組織經濟體、能源工作組及相關附屬機制、有關國際組織和研究機構約50名代表參加會議。（國家能源局）

### 2026-07-31 19:16:58  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Brent #Impact bullish #XOM.N #Impact mixed

【Exxon says Middle East growth plan intact despite ~500k bpd outage from Iran conflict】Exxon Mobil (XOM.N) says it remains committed to Middle East investment despite roughly 500,000 bpd of production offline after conflict tied to Iran. CFO Neil Hansen: "We will not make broad investment decisions based on today's headlines. We can thrive in this environment and remain committed to current and future investments in the region." Before the late-February fighting, Exxon's UAE and Qatar crude output totaled about 900,000 bpd, roughly one-fifth of its global production; those volumes are now heavily disrupted. Two Qatar gas projects in which Exxon holds stakes were badly damaged in an Iran strike, leaving about 450,000 bpd of Qatar output suspended and roughly 50,000 bpd in the UAE affected. Hansen said some firms view staying away from the region as an advantage; Exxon calls that short-sighted. Some UAE volumes are being held in storage pending the lifting of transport restrictions.

### 2026-07-31 19:04:28  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

India Fiscal Deficit Widens — India's fiscal deficit surged to INR 3.1 trillion in the first quarter of FY2027 from INR 280 billion in the corresponding period of the previous year. This was equivalent to 28.7% of the current budget estimate, which is commonly depleted toward the end of the fiscal year, compared to 26.7% in the previous year. As a net energy importer, the country is grappling with rising fuel subsidy costs due to elevated oil prices linked to the ongoing Middle East conflict. Total expenditure surged 11.0% year-on-year to INR 13.5 trillion (25.4% of budgeted amount), while total receipts rose by 11.5 to INR 10-5 trillion (28.7% of the budgeted amount).

【蘿蔔快跑：部分網路賬號惡意製造誤導性內容，公司已提起訴訟】7月31日，蘿蔔快跑法務部發布宣告：近期，我們注意到，部分網路賬號透過拼接無事實關聯的畫面、錄音，惡意製造涉“蘿蔔快跑”誤導性內容，涉嫌侵犯蘿蔔快跑合法權益。經核查，抖音賬號“其實太陽**”將來源於其他事件的司機哭訴錄音與蘿蔔快跑相關畫面進行拼接傳播，引導公眾誤認為相關事件由蘿蔔快跑引發；抖音賬號“金*”則將因其他事件引發的司機聚集畫面與蘿蔔快跑進行惡意拼接，並使用誤導性標題引發公眾誤解。相關內容釋出後，引發大量互動和傳播。上述內容透過拼接無事實關聯的畫面、錄音，製造蘿蔔快跑與相關事件之間的不實關聯，涉嫌誤導公眾認知，已超出理性討論和正常監督範疇。針對上述行為，我司已依法向人民法院提起訴訟，目前相關案件均已獲法院立案。我們將持續關注各類涉蘿蔔快跑侵權內容傳播情況，並依法採取必要措施，維護自身合法權益。

【AI生產力應用ARR約6.2億元 美圖股價漲超10%】今日，美圖公司盤中漲超15%，截至收盤，報4.72港元/股，漲10.28%，成交額近10億港元。 訊息面上，美圖公司於前一日預告2026年中期業績。按非國際財務報告準則，2026年上半年，美圖經調整歸母淨利潤預計同比增長36%至40%，高於瑞銀等機構此前預期；影像與設計產品收入約18億元，同比增長30.9%。 相比利潤增長本身，美圖新品的ARR（年度經常性收入）也成為披露資料的關鍵指標之一。公告顯示，截至2026年6月，美圖AI生產力應用ARR約6.2億元，同比增長47.8%。其中，開拍ARR同比增長超過100%，Vmake同比增長超過200%，今年新推出的MVLAND ARR在上線三個月內實現翻倍。（新浪科技）

【中國氣象局：加快推動人工智慧與氣象業務深度融合】記者31日從中國氣象局瞭解到，氣象部門正加快推動人工智慧與氣象業務深度融合，積極推進有組織的系統性研發和應用。中國氣象局近日召開數值預報和人工智慧氣象應用工作機制專題會議，強調當前氣象領域人工智慧技術迭代提速、國際格局深度調整，風險挑戰前所未有。氣象人工智慧發展面臨激烈競爭的同時，人工智慧產業正從“煉模型”到“拼落地”轉變。當前，人工智慧與氣象業務的深度融合，是關乎氣象業務效能躍升、服務模式重塑、防災效能倍增的戰略性選擇。氣象部門將加快推進氣象人工智慧發展和氣象智慧預警方案“媽祖”出海工作。堅持一張藍圖繪到底，加快推動氣象人工智慧高質量發展。（新華社）

### 2026-07-31 18:41:31  #宏觀

【上海市委網信辦聯合多部門對汽車類“自媒體”開展合規指導】據“網信上海”，為深入推進規範汽車行業網上資訊傳播秩序專項整治行動，根據日常巡查和網民舉報線索，7月30日，上海市委網信辦聯合市經信委、市市場監管局、市公安局網安總隊等部門召開專題會議，對一批粉絲量大、內容生產覆蓋汽車產業鏈上下游多領域、有一定影響力的汽車類“自媒體”（總計覆蓋粉絲量逾500萬）開展合規指導。會議指出，當前上海汽車產業正處在向電動化、智慧化轉型的關鍵期，規範汽車行業網上資訊傳播秩序、淨化汽車領域網路輿論生態，既是對汽車產業和消費者合法權益的保護，也是對“自媒體”行業健康發展的積極促進。汽車類“自媒體”要嚴格遵守《網路安全法》《網路資訊內容生態治理規定》《網路測評活動規範》《汽車行業價格行為合規指南》等法律法規和行業規範，不得釋出惡意唱衰市場、歪曲解讀政策、違規營銷引流、引導非理性維權等資訊，不得以虛構事實、夾帶隱性商業合作等違規網路測評誤導消費者，不得以文不對題、誇大宣傳等煽動性標題博取關注、惡意引流變現。參會的汽車類“自媒體”運營主體表示，將主動加強相關法律法規學習，對照專項整治要求開展自查自糾，確保內容生產依法合規、有序運營。下一步，上海市委網信辦將會同相關部門，持續加大對汽車類“自媒體”亂象的整治力度，不斷淨化汽車領域網路輿論生態。歡迎廣大網民透過“規範汽車行業網上資訊傳播秩序”專項舉報入口積極舉報違規線索，共同營造清朗網路空間。

【財政部召開新修改註冊會計師法貫徹落實座談會】2026年7月28日，財政部召開新修改註冊會計師法貫徹落實座談會。財政部黨組成員、副部長郭婷婷出席會議並講話。全國人大常委會法工委副主任雷建斌應邀出席會議並介紹了修法總體情況和重點內容。 會議指出，新修改註冊會計師法深入貫徹習近平總書記重要指示批示精神和黨中央決策部署，從加強黨的領導、完善監管措施、加大責任追究力度、支援促進註冊會計師行業健康發展等方面作了重點修改，為確保行業正確發展方向提供了法治保障，為打擊財務造假、加強財會監督提供了堅實支撐，為發揮行業專業價值提供了重要指引。 會議強調，註冊會計師行業在維護資本市場秩序和社會公眾利益、提升會計資訊質量和經濟效率等方面發揮著重要作用，要緊扣服務國家建設這個主題，堅持誠信建設這條主線，不斷提升執業質量，發揮“看門人”作用，在服務國家發展大局中推動行業高質量發展。 會議強調，要紮實做好新修改註冊會計師法的貫徹落實工作。系統開展宣傳貫徹，做到全面覆蓋、突出重點，推動新修改註冊會計師法在行業內人人知曉、主動落實。加快完善配套制度，做好與有關法律制度的銜接安排，確保將法律要求落實落細。持續加強監督管理，提升監管效率和水平，依法加大責任追究力度。促進行業健康發展，不斷提升規範化、專業化水平，引領行業向價值鏈高階延伸。(財政部)

### 2026-07-31 18:34:55  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK #FTSE100 #Impact bullish

【UK regulator sets scope for consolidated UK equity trade data】The Financial Conduct Authority on Friday published the scope of UK equity trading data it will permit to be released, a move aimed at boosting liquidity on the London Stock Exchange. The FCA said it plans to introduce a single data source as early as next year that will publish both pre-trade and post-trade data, enabling investors to compare quotes across venues before execution. The regulator is consulting on whether to include investment bank trading data and on how revenue from the product should be allocated. As a transitional measure, the FCA will from Friday publish a daily closing consolidated UK equity report, the Market Activity Report (MARS), containing the past 12 months of data to help market participants assess market size.

### 2026-07-31 18:27:57  #國際

【哈馬斯高官：是否解除武裝取決於以色列從加沙撤軍進展】巴勒斯坦伊斯蘭抵抗運動（哈馬斯）高階官員加齊·哈馬德7月31日在接受半島電視臺等媒體採訪時說，哈馬斯是否解除武裝取決於以色列從加沙地帶撤軍和加沙重建工作的進展。哈馬德說，哈馬斯將移交武器，由加沙國家行政委員會負責保管。加沙國家行政委員會和國際穩定部隊必須進入加沙地帶，以色列支援的武裝民兵必須解散。哈馬德說，談判十分艱難。如果以色列軍隊不履行其義務，哈馬斯將不會執行協議的任何部分。美國總統特朗普30日在社交媒體發文稱，已達成一項關於“全面解除”哈馬斯武裝的協議。特朗普說，該協議將分階段實施，隨著解除武裝工作的完成，以色列軍隊將撤出加沙地帶，而國際穩定部隊將與新組建的巴勒斯坦警察部隊協作，共同確保加沙地帶居民及其周邊地區的安全。(新華社)
